Section 2RURAL DEVELOPMENT AND PANCHAYAT RAJ DEPARTMENTA Government Order and Notification were issued for the restructuring and upgradation of the Harur Town Panchayat—by merging with it two Village Panchayats situated within the Harur Panchayat Union in Dharmapuri District—into a new Municipal Council. Accordingly, an Order is hereby issued, and a Notification is published in accordance with Section 15, Sub-section (4)(i) of the Tamil Nadu Panchayats Act, 1994 (Tamil Nadu Act 21/1994), for the restructuring of rural local bodies by excluding the two aforementioned Village Panchayats from the Harur Panchayat Union.Rural Development and Panchayat Raj DepartmentNotification No. II(2)/RDPR/220(w)/2026,--G.O.Ms.No. 71, Rural Development and Panchayat [Para-2(1)] Department, dated 13th March 2026.19113-03-2026Part II - Section 2Notifications or Orders of interest to a Section of the public issued by Secretariat DepartmentsA Government Order and Notification were issued for the upgradation and restructuring of Namakkal Municipality into the Namakkal Municipal Corporation, by integrating 12 Village Panchayats drawn from the Erumapatti, Mohanur, Puduchatram, and Namakkal Panchayat Unions within Namakkal District. Consequently, an Order and Notification are hereby issued—in accordance with Section 15, Sub-section (4)(i) of the Tamil Nadu Panchayats Act, 1994 (Tamil Nadu Act 21/1994)—effecting the restructuring of Rural Local Bodies by excluding the said 12 Village Panchayats situated within the Erumapatti, Mohanur, Puduchatram, and Namakkal Panchayat Unions.Rural Development and Panchayat Raj DepartmentNotificaton No. II(2)/RDPR/220(v-1)/2026, Notification No. II(2)/RDPR/ 220(v-2)/2026, Notification No. II(2)/RDPR/220(v-3)/2026, Notification No. II(2)/RDPR/220(v-4)/2026.--G.O. Ms. No. 70, Rural Development and Panchayat [Para-2(1)] Department, dated 13th March 2026.19013-03-2026Part II - Section 2Notifications or Orders of interest to a Section of the public issued by Secretariat DepartmentsA Government Order and Notification were issued to upgrade and restructure the Pudukkottai Municipality into the Pudukkottai Municipal Corporation by fully integrating 8 Village Panchayats—and partially integrating 3 Village Panchayats—from the Thiruvarankulam, Pudukkottai, Annavasal, and Arimalam Panchayat Unions within the Pudukkottai District. Consequently, an Order and Notification are hereby issued, in accordance with Section 15, Sub-section (4)(i) of the Tamil Nadu Panchayats Act, 1994 (Tamil Nadu Act 21/1994), to restructure the Rural Local Bodies by removing—fully in the case of 8 Village Panchayats and partially in the case of 3 Village Panchayats—those entities situated within the Thiruvarankulam, Pudukkottai, Annavasal, and Arimalam Panchayat Unions.Rural Development and Panchayat Raj DepartmentNotification No. II(2)/RDPR/220(u-1)/2026, Notification No. II(2)/RDPR/220(u-2)/2026, Notification No. II(2)/RDPR/220(u-3)/2026, Notification No. II(2)/RDPR/220(u-4)/2026.--G.O. Ms No. 69, Rural Development and Panchayat [Para-2(1)] Department, dated 13th March 202618913-03-2026Part II - Section 2Notifications or Orders of interest to a Section of the public issued by Secretariat DepartmentsA Government Order and Notification have been issued for the upgradation and restructuring of the administrative framework, whereby 18 Village Panchayats—drawn from the Thurinjapuran and Tiruvannamalai Panchayat Unions in the Tiruvannamalai District—have been merged with the Tiruvannamalai Municipality to constitute the Tiruvannamalai Municipal Corporation. Consequently, an Order and Notification are hereby issued, in accordance with Section 15, Sub-section (4)(i) of the Tamil Nadu Panchayats Act, 1994 (Tamil Nadu Act 21/1994), for the restructuring of Rural Local Bodies through the exclusion of the said 18 Village Panchayats from the Thurinjapuran and Tiruvannamalai Panchayat Unions.Rural Development and Panchayat Raj DepartmentNotification No. II(2)/RDPR/220(t-1)/2026, Notification No. II(2)/RDPR/220(t-2)/2026.--G.O. (CLA/TNP/2025/04/06/03/C/0001)
No. VI(1)/406(c-1)/2026.
The Government of Tamil Nadu having been satisfied that the lands specified in the schedule below to be acquired 
for industrial purpose to wit to Tnpdcl - Super Critical Thermal Power Project Stage II and III Udangudi and it having 
already been decided that the amount of compensation to be awarded for the lands are to be paid out of the funds 
controlled or managed by, Tnpdcl The following notice is issued under sub-section(1) of section 3 of the Tamil Nadu 
Acquisition of Land for Industrial Purposes Act, 1997. (Tamil Nadu Act 10 of 1999)
NOTICE
Under sub-section(1) of section 3 of the Tamil Nadu Acquisition of Lands for Industrial Purposes Act, 1997 
(Tamil Nadu Act 10 of 1999), the Governor of Tamil Nadu hereby acquires the lands specified in the Schedule below 
measuring an extent of 8.0450 hectares to the same, a little more or less needed for industrial purpose wit, for Tnpdcl - 
Super Critical Thermal Power Project Stage II and III Udangudi.
The plan of the lands are kept in the office of the Special DRO(LA), Thoothukkudi and Special Tahsildar(LA), 
Thoothukkudi may be inspected on any day during office hours.

(CLA-TNP/2026/04/04/02/0001)
No. VI(1)/406(c-14)/2026.
 The Government of Tamil Nadu having been satisfied that the lands specified in the schedule below to be acquired 
for industrial purpose to wit to Tnpdcl- Super Critical Thermal Power Project Stage II and III Udangudi and it having 
already been decided that the amount of compensation to be awarded for the lands are to be paid out of the funds 
controlled or managed by, Tnpdcl. The following notice is issued under sub-section(1) of section 3 of the Tamil Nadu 
Acquisition of Land for Industrial Purposes Act, 1997. (Tamil Nadu Act 10 of 1999)
NOTICE
Under sub-section(1) of section 3 of the Tamil Nadu Acquisition of Lands for Industrial Purposes Act, 1997 
(Tamil Nadu Act 10 of 1999), the Governor of Tamil Nadu hereby acquires the lands specified in the Schedule below 
measuring an extent of 11.7450 hectares to the same, a little more or less needed for industrial purpose wit, for 
Tnpdcl. - Super Critical Thermal Power Project Stage II and III Udangudi.
The plan of the lands are kept in the office of the Special DRO(LA), Thoothukkudi and Special Tahsildar(LA), 
Thoothukkudi which may be inspected on any day during office hours.

(CLA-TNP/2026/04/01/01/0001)
No. VI(1)/406(c-15)/2026.
The Government of Tamil Nadu having been satisfied that the lands specified in the schedule below to be acquired 
for industrial purpose to wit to Tnpdol - Super Critical Thermal Power Project Stage II and III Udangudi and it having 
already been decided that the amount of compensation to be awarded for the lands are to be paid out of the funds 
controlled or managed by, Tnpdol. The following notice is issued under sub-section(1) of section 3 of the Tamil Nadu 
Acquisition of Land for Industrial Purposes Act, 1997. (Tamil Nadu Act 10 of 1999)
NOTICE
Under sub-section (1) of Section 3 of the Tamil Nadu Acquisition of Lands for Industrial Purposes Act, 1997 
(Tamil Nadu Act 10 of 1999), the Governor of Tamil Nadu hereby acquires the lands specified in the Schedule below 
measuring an extent of 10.2000 hectares to the same, a little more or less needed for industrial purpose wit, for 
Tnpdol - Super Critical Thermal Power Project Stage II and III Udangudi.
The plan of the lands are kept in the office of the Special DRO(LA), Thoothukkudi and Special Tahsildar(LA), 
Thoothukkudi which may be inspected on any day during office hours.
